- **imap-email**: Read emails via IMAP. Use when the user wants to check their inbox, read unread emails, search emails, or view specific emails. - **imap-expert**: Deep expertise on IMAP email protocol and server administration, grounded in "Managing IMAP" by Dianna Mullet and Kevin Mullet (O'Reilly). Covers current implementations: Dovecot, Cyrus IMAP, Exchange/Office 365, hosted providers. Use when asked about: IMAP vs POP3 differences, mailbox/folder management, flags and UIDs, IMAP namespaces, IMAP capabilities, configuring or tuning Dovecot, Cyrus IMAP, Courier, or Exchange IMAP; IMAP client configuration (Thunderbird, Apple Mail, Outlook, iOS/Android), SASL authentication, TLS/ SSL, STARTTLS, OAuth2 for IMAP, IMAP IDLE, IMAP quota, SORT/THREAD ext… ([Wbunker/skills-repo](https://github.com/Wbunker/skills-repo/tree/HEAD/imap-expert) · ★1)
- **imap-idle**: Event-driven email monitoring using IMAP IDLE protocol. Replaces polling with instant push notifications via OpenClaw webhooks. Use when setting up email monitoring, replacing hourly email checks, or implementing event-driven email processing. Monitors multiple IMAP accounts, triggers webhooks on new mail, zero tokens while waiting. ([lxyeternal/MalSkillBench](https://github.com/lxyeternal/MalSkillBench/tree/HEAD/Dataset/Skills/benign/imap-idle) · ★67 · 10 copies)
- **imap-job-search**: Methodology for AI-led job search over the mailbox (UNIFY). Use when running or planning a jobber search loop — finding job-alert emails via the protonesk MCP, extracting postings via the jobber MCP, scoring fit, and persisting to the idae-machine store. Carries the non-code knowledge: per-board sender domains & subjects, IMAP query recipes, digest handling, cheap pre-filters, referential mapping (job_source/seniority/contract_type/skill), and re-run cadence / dedup. Triggers on "search jobs", "check job alerts", "run jobber", "new job matches", IMAP job recipes. ([medyll/jobber](https://github.com/medyll/jobber/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/imap-job-search))
- **imap-job-search**: Methodology for AI-led email job search (UNIFY). Use when the user wants to find/triage job offers from their mailbox. Orchestrates the protonesk (mail), jobber (compute) and idae (store) MCP servers — search job-alert emails, extract postings, score fit vs profile, dedupe and persist to MongoDB, surface ranked matches. Triggers on "find jobs", "check job alerts", "run job search", "score new offers", "recherche d'emploi". ([medyll/jobber](https://github.com/medyll/jobber/tree/HEAD/skills/imap-job-search))

- **ime-lexicon-bridge**: 素材库 ↔ 创作词库 ↔ 画像：harvest 用去 AI 味筛子抽专属词， guide 出用词包，acquire 按画像缺口出搜索计划。只读，不 apply。 触发：抽创作词、用词包、金句抽词、写之前看词、素材库词库打通、画像补词。 ([taxueseek/ime-lexicon](https://github.com/taxueseek/ime-lexicon/tree/HEAD/skill/sub-skills/bridge) · ★16)

- **imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-aggregation-gen**: HIS 服务总线（db_hsb.sm_hsb）生产者/消费者绑定索引构建与查询。用 kingbase-87-aggregation MCP 导出接入系统、注册服务、发布服务、消息订阅与路由证据，推断并归一化为「HIS ↔ 外部系统」的绑定关系，并把 HIS 发布的绑定挂接到代码调用图的 Controller 方法上（改动 Controller 可反查受影响厂家接口），输出 aggregation_db.json / aggregation_raw.json / aggregation_stats.json。 Use when 需要回答某个外部系统与 HIS 之间是谁生产、谁消费、走 REST 还是消息、绑定证据是什么；首次生成或服务总线登记变化（新增服务、发布/注册端点、消费组、消息路由）后重建索引；aggregation_db.json 缺失或过期。 只读总线库，不写库、不改配置；纯查询用 query_aggregation.py。 ([SkylerCook/imedical.agents](https://github.com/SkylerCook/imedical.agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph/skills/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-aggregation-gen))
- **imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-front-gen**: HIS 医生站前端链路索引构建与增量更新。扫描 static 下全部 HTML/JS，建立「界面 → 操作元素 → JS 方法 → HTTP 请求」链路，输出 front_chain_db.json / front_chains.jsonl；可选导入 Kingbase 菜单快照为页面补充业务角色；服务端配置下发的按钮/菜单按场景登记并从数据库导出快照。 Use when 首次初始化前端链路索引、前端页面或脚本变化后重建/增量更新、front_chain_db.json 缺失或过期、需要查询某个按钮最终调用了哪些后端接口、或需要评估前端改造的影响范围。 本期只输出 URL 路径，不关联后端 Controller。 ([SkylerCook/imedical.agents](https://github.com/SkylerCook/imedical.agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph/skills/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-front-gen))
- **imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-gen**: HIS 医生站代码调用图索引构建与增量更新。执行流水线：Feign 接口扫描 → 工作区方法级调用图 → Maven biz jar 扫描 → 合并 → Controller 注解端点索引（controller_endpoints.json）→ 类型嵌套索引（type_nesting.json）→ 类型使用索引（type_usage.json）→ 影响分析索引（impact_db.json）→ 两部分穿透数据库（penetration_db.json）。 Use when 首次初始化 codegraph 数据、拉取子仓库或升级 Maven 依赖后重建/增量更新索引、impact_db.json / penetration_db.json 缺失或过期、或需要重新生成调用图数据。 纯查询与影响分析请使用 imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-search。 ([SkylerCook/imedical.agents](https://github.com/SkylerCook/imedical.agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph/skills/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-gen))
- **imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-search**: HIS 医生站代码调用图检索。基于已生成的 impact_db.json / penetration_db.json / 调用图 JSONL / 前端链路库，提供变更影响分析（6 段式）、方法调用者与被调用者查询、Feign 接口使用方查询、跨服务依赖追踪、提交级批量取证（周报联动）、前后端链路衔接（HTML 页面 → 操作元素 → JS 方法 → HTTP 请求 → 后端 Controller），后端方法 → 受影响按钮（含经 Feign 间接入口），以及提交级入口 → 界面 → 菜单访问位置联动（结合前端界面入口登记）。 Use when 查谁调用了某方法、某方法影响哪些下游、某 Controller 对应哪个 Feign 接口、某 Feign 被哪些工作区服务或 Maven 模块调用、需要按服务/模块汇总影响范围、给定一批 git 提交需要结构化的入口/调用方/下游 Feign 证据、需要从某个 HTML 页面/按钮/URL 追到后端 Controller 的完整链路、需要从改动的方法反查受影响的按钮、或需要把提交入口批量落到界面与菜单访问位置。 索引未生成或已过期时，先用 imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-gen 构建；前端链路库缺失时先用 imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-front-gen 构建。 ([SkylerCook/imedical.agents](https://github.com/SkylerCook/imedical.agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph/skills/imedicalxc-doctor-codegraph-search))
- **imedicalxc-doctor-commit-report**: HIS 医生站信创产品迭代周报。采集 src/ 下 20 个子仓库在指定时间窗内 11 位医生站同事的 git 提交，输出五要素汇总（岗位工作站 / 需求类型 / 优先级 / 提交人 / 改造内容总结），评估前经 codegraph 调用图批量取证（改动方法 → 入口 / 工作区调用方 / 外部 Maven 调用方 / 下游 Feign → 前端界面 / 操作元素 / 菜单访问位置，结构化 JSON），再对【流程修改 / 业务调整】类提交做混合式评估（轻量全覆盖 + 高风险三类自动升级深度评估，1-5 星评级，报告期只给初评、测试用例 / 联调用例产出后回评定档，1-2★ 必须给出至少一条具体问题评价，测试建议四段式且标注界面、访问位置、操作步骤并可追溯到证据），并自动检测数据库脚本执行提醒（PO 数据结构修改 / 新增系统标准配置 / 新增系统标准提醒 / 新增代码表缓存配置），归档 Markdown 报告，将 1-2★ 低星问题经 tencent_docs MCP 登记到《信创版产品迭代每周问题登记》（多个独立问题的提交按问题拆行），并按配置尝试推送。用于周度定时汇总或按需汇总提交记录。 ([SkylerCook/imedical.agents](https://github.com/SkylerCook/imedical.agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/imedicalxc-doctor-commit-report/skills/imedicalxc-doctor-commit-report))

- **imerg-fetch**: Fetch NASA IMERG daily precipitation (global 0.1° grid) for a date range from NASA GES DISC via Earthdata login, as a weather-skills Zarr. Serves the daily Late (GPM_3IMERGDL, 1998 to 1–2 days ago) and Final (GPM_3IMERGDF, gauge-adjusted research quality, months behind real time) files. Use only for IMERG Final. For IMERG Late or Early, recent rainfall, or any day after Final ends, use dynamical-fetch instead: it needs no login, reads only the box, and covers 1998 to now. ([rhiza-research/rhiza-weather-skills](https://github.com/rhiza-research/rhiza-weather-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/imerg-fetch) · ★6 · 2 copies)

- **imessage**: Read and send iMessages via local macOS capabilities (no third-party API). Two modes. Send mode drafts a message and uses AppleScript (`osascript`) on explicit "send it" and requires only Automation, Messages permission (scoped, revocable). Read mode pulls from `~/Library/Messages/chat.db` via sqlite3 and requires Full Disk Access for Terminal, which is a broad permission, so it is opt-in only. Trigger on phrases like "text [person]", "iMessage [person]", "draft an imessage", "read my texts from [person]", "what did [person] text me", "/imessage". Defaults to drafts-only, never auto-sends.
